SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.step
  {β : Fin 8 → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRationalInterval.Interval} {κ τ : ℚ} {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate β κ τ N) {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  (hb : ∀ (i : Fin 8), (β i).Contains (b i)) (i : Fin N) {x : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.State}
  (hx : (c.steps i).input.Contains x) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.ActivePhase (↑τ) b (↑κ) (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.pack x) ∧
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.NativeLegal (↑τ) b (↑κ) (1 / 2)
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.pack x) ∧
      (c.steps i).output.Contains
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.f b
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.pack x)))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.enclosed
  {β : Fin 8 → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRationalInterval.Interval} {κ τ : ℚ} {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate β κ τ N) {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  (hb : ∀ (i : Fin 8), (β i).Contains (b i)) (i : Fin (N + 1)) :
  (c.boxes i).Contains
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.orbit b ↑i))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.guards
  {β : Fin 8 → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRationalInterval.Interval} {κ τ : ℚ} {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate β κ τ N) {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  (hb : ∀ (i : Fin 8), (β i).Contains (b i)) (i : Fin N) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.ActivePhase (↑τ) b (↑κ)
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.orbit b ↑i) ∧
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.NativeLegal (↑τ) b (↑κ) (1 / 2)
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.orbit b ↑i)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.unit
  {β : Fin 8 → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRationalInterval.Interval} {κ τ : ℚ} {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate β κ τ N) {b : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  (hb : ∀ (i : Fin 8), (β i).Contains (b i)) (i : Fin (N + 1)) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexNormSq
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.orbit b ↑i) =
    1
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.inverse_arithmetic :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundIntervalReciprocal.Arithmetic
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRationalInterval.Interval.point SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.rawNorm)
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.K
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.initial_inclusion :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeBox.normalizedBox SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePrefixInitial.rawBox
        SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.K).Included
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.initialBox
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.original_seed_enclosed :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefixInitial.initialBox.Contains
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.initial)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox_sound {B A : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeBox.StateBox}
  {x u : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.State} (hx : B.Contains x) (hu : A.Contains u) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox B A).Contains (x - u)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.coordinate_distance
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundUncertainNativeBasin.Certificate) {B A : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeBox.StateBox}
  {x u : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.State} {δ : ℚ} (hδ : 0 ≤ δ) (hx : B.Contains x) (hu : A.Contains u)
  (hrows :
    ∀ (i : Fin 8),
      ((c.coordinateBox (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox B A)).R i).bound ≤ δ ∧
        ((c.coordinateBox (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox B A)).I i).bound ≤ δ) :
  dist (c.T.symm x) (c.T.symm u) ≤ ↑δ
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.negativeTargetBox_sound {z : Fin 9 → ℚ} {r : ℚ}
  {u : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.Field}
  (hu : ∀ (i : Fin 8), (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundBackgroundKernel.fieldBox z r i).Contains (u i)) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.negativeTargetBox z r).Contains (-u, 0)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.iterate_tendsto_of_tail
  (f : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField)
  (z y : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField) (N : ℕ)
  (h : Filter.Tendsto (fun n => f^[n] (f^[N] z)) Filter.atTop (nhds y)) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n => f^[n] z) Filter.atTop (nhds y)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.iterate_property_of_tail
  (f : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField → SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField)
  (z : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField) (P : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.ComplexField → Prop) (N : ℕ)
  (hp : ∀ (i : Fin N), P (f^[↑i] z)) (ht : ∀ (n : ℕ), P (f^[n] (f^[N] z))) (n : ℕ) : P (f^[n] z)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.background_sound (i : Fin 18) (j : Fin 8) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.β i j).Contains (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i j)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.prefix_guards (i : Fin 18) {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.Prefix i N) (j : Fin N) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.ActivePhase SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i) (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.κ i))
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i ↑j) ∧
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.NativeLegal SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i) (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.κ i))
      (1 / 2) (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i ↑j)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.distance_from_boxes (i : Fin 18) {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.Prefix i N) {δ : ℚ} (hδ : 0 ≤ δ)
  (hrows :
    ∀ (j : Fin 8),
      (((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.data i).c.coordinateBox
                  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox
                    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.boxes c (Fin.last N))
                    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.negativeTargetBox
                      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).z
                      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).r))).R
              j).bound ≤
          δ ∧
        (((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.data i).c.coordinateBox
                  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox
                    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.boxes c (Fin.last N))
                    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.negativeTargetBox
                      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).z
                      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).r))).I
              j).bound ≤
          δ) :
  dist
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i N)))
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0)) ≤
    ↑δ
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.rounded_seed_return (i : Fin 18) {N : ℕ}
  (c : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.Prefix i N)
  (hentry :
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i N)) ∈
      Metric.closedBall
        ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
          (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0))
        ↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.data i).ρ) :
  Filter.Tendsto (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i) Filter.atTop
      (nhds
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.realEmbed (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1))) ∧
    ∀ (n : ℕ),
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.ActivePhase SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i) (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.κ i))
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i n) ∧
        SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.NativeLegal SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i) (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.κ i))
            (1 / 2) (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i n) ∧
          SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexNormSq (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i n) = 1
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.rounded_seed_geometric_tail (i : Fin 18) {N : ℕ}
  (hentry :
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i N)) ∈
      Metric.closedBall
        ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
          (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0))
        ↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.data i).ρ)
  (n : ℕ) :
  dist
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i (n + N))))
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0)) ≤
    ↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.rate i) ^ n *
      dist
        ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i N)))
        ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
          (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0))

SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.entry_times (i : Fin 18) :
  15 ≤ (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).N ∧
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).N ≤ 65
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.rounded_original_seed_return (i : Fin 18) :
  Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n =>
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate
              SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
              (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
              (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ) (1 / 2))^[n]
          SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.initial)
      Filter.atTop
      (nhds
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.realEmbed (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1))) ∧
    ∀ (n : ℕ),
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.ActivePhase SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
          (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ)
          ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate
                SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
                (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
                (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ) (1 / 2))^[n]
            SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.initial) ∧
        SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.NativeLegal SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
            (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ) (1 / 2)
            ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate
                  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
                  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
                  (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ) (1 / 2))^[n]
              SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.initial) ∧
          SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.complexNormSq
              ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeIteration.nativeUpdate
                    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativeControls.originalTolerance
                    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.rounded_b i)
                    (↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.certificates i).κ) (1 / 2))^[n]
                SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalSeed.initial) =
            1
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.rounded_original_geometric_tail (i : Fin 18) (n : ℕ) :
  dist
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack
          (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i
            (n + (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).N))))
      ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.T i).symm
        (-(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1, 0)) ≤
    ↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.rate i) ^ n *
      ↑(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).δ
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.all_original_prefix_enclosed (i : Fin 18)
  (j : Fin ((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).N + 1)) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativePrefix.Certificate.boxes
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.data i).c j).Contains
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundNativePhase.unpack (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i ↑j))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.rounded_original_density_limit (i : Fin 18) (j : Fin 8) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n => Complex.normSq (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit i n j)) Filter.atTop
    (nhds
      (Complex.normSq
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.realEmbed (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsets.roundedRoot i).1 j)))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.eventual_original_density_separation :
  ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
    Complex.normSq (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit 0 n 0) + 9 / 100 <
      Complex.normSq (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetSeedReturn.orbit 17 n 0)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetSeed.zero_entry_budget_rejected :
  ¬(((SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalOffsetBasins.data 0).c.coordinateBox
              (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundCertifiedNativeEntry.differenceBox
                SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefix.Point000.entryBox
                SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOffsetPrefix.Point000.targetBox)).R
          0).bound ≤
      0
